  queue-service: the document-precheck queue
    ✓ initQueues creates document-precheck with the brief's job options and a sandboxed processor file — even though pdf-processor.js is absent (5 ms)
    ✓ a job that failed its LAST attempt is marked FAILED; a failure with a retry left is not (4 ms)
    ✓ a job Bull failed for stalling past its limit (attemptsMade < attempts, isFailed true) is marked FAILED (3 ms)
    ✓ when Redis cannot say the job state, the stall message still marks FAILED and a retry-left failure still does not (3 ms)
    ✓ a `failed` event with no job (Bull could not load it) is ignored without throwing (2 ms)
  POST /draft-documents — the pre-check hook
    ✓ an in-scope upload creates a PENDING pre-check for exactly this document and adds the job (54 ms)
    ✓ enqueue-failure-still-uploads: the queue add throws → 200 with the unchanged body, and the row is FAILED with the failure flag (25 ms)
    ✓ I2: a queue add that never settles (unreachable Redis) → the route answers 200 unchanged within ~2 s, and the row is FAILED (2026 ms)
    ✓ even enqueueForUpload itself throwing (database down) leaves the upload response unchanged (22 ms)
    ✓ an out-of-scope slot uploads exactly as before and creates no pre-check (21 ms)
